# E2-T07 · Universal list, filter-values, saved views and synchronous exports (S03 WP-03-C)

## Objective
Work package WP-03-C: one reusable list engine for every backoffice list (D5, D15 now; every later list).

## Spec references
`docs/specs/00-transversal/CONVENCIONS_API.md` §3–5 (list contract, operators per type, `filter-values`, `fields`, sort, page limits, `INVALID_FILTER`) · S03 R-03-22 (filter-values), R-03-23 (saved views per user and `listKey`), R-03-24 (exports: xlsx/pdf, `columns=`, `202` when > 5 000 rows), `ExportPolicy` (INSTRUCTOR → 403 per A17a; IBAN masked) · tests **T-03-08, 09, 10, 11**.

## Context to load
CONVENCIONS_API §3–5 · S03 R-03-22…R-03-24, §6 rows `/members`, `/members/filter-values`, `/members/export`, `/saved-views` · `docs/DECISIONS_PENDENTS.md` A17 · this file.

## Steps
1. `ListQuery` parser (`page,size,sort,q,filter=<field>:<op>:<value>` repeatable, `fields`) → Mongo criteria from a per-endpoint `ListDefinition` (filterable fields with types/operators, sortable, searchable text fields, default sort); unknown field/operator → `400 INVALID_FILTER`; page size caps.
2. `filter-values?field=` facets (value + count, top 50, respecting the other active filters).
3. `SavedView` (per account + `listKey`: name, columns, filters, sort; CRUD endpoints).
4. Synchronous export (`format=xlsx|pdf`, `columns=`): Apache POI (xlsx) and a simple PDF renderer with the club brand; ≤ 5 000 rows inline, otherwise `202 {jobId}` handing over to E2-T08; `ExportPolicy` (role, masked fields); event `DataExported` + audit `DATA_EXPORTED`.
5. Apply the engine to `/members` and `/dogs` (E2-T06 controllers).

## Verification (run all; paste full output in the report)
- `./mvnw -q verify` → green with T-03-08…11 (parser cases, facets, saved views, export inline vs 202, INSTRUCTOR 403).

## Definition of Done
Engine reusable (documented in `docs/playbooks/new-endpoint.md` addendum «list endpoints»); exports masked.

## Evidence to return
`verify` output; a `curl` with two filters and a sort on `/members`.

---

## Executor report
Implemented E2-T07 in the working tree. The final Maven verification, OpenAPI regeneration, real HTTP curl smoke and PDF visual check pass.

### Branch / commits
Working tree only. No git write commands were run. The publish script owns the commit/push after this session.

### Evidence (full command outputs)
Maven uses `MAVEN_USER_HOME=/tmp/e1-t09-maven` and `MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/e1-t09-maven/repository` because the default caches are read-only. Docker Desktop was available. Outputs below are complete; token/hash values are truncated under AGENTS.md and line-ending/trailing whitespace is normalized for Markdown. The first full verification exposed a new curl fixture with incomplete Account.Security; the fixture now initializes it and uses the registered `clubs-admin` client. Earlier focused iterations also corrected duplicate fixture IDs, native PDF QA headless mode, and word wrapping.

`MAVEN_USER_HOME=/tmp/e1-t09-maven MAVEN_OPTS=-Dmaven.repo.local=/tmp/e1-t09-maven/repository ./mvnw -q clean` completed with no output (exit 0) before the full build cycle.

### Rules and tests implemented (R-xx-nn / T-xx-nn)
- R-03-22 / T-03-08: reusable typed `ListQuery`/`ListDefinition`, literal text search, page sizes/caps, deterministic multi-sort, sparse fields, repeated AND filters, all Mongo operators, member/dog joins and role/module/tenant restrictions. Real curl returns 15 records for two filters and descending surname order.
- R-03-22 / T-03-09: facets apply q and other-field filters, count each document once per value, order by count/value, cap at 50 and localize Plan/Level labels with club fallback.
- R-03-23 / T-03-10: saved-view CRUD, owner/list/club uniqueness, own/shared reads, owner-or-admin writes/deletes, stale versions, unknown-column removal on load, field/role validation and cross-tenant isolation.
- R-03-24 / T-03-11 / A17a: ADMIN-only XLSX/PDF, selected ordered columns, pagination-independent export, safe projections and IBAN masking, text cells preventing formulas, inline through 5000 rows and durable QUEUED handoff at 5001. Catalog DataExported and @Audited(DATA_EXPORTED) commit together; the rollback test proves neither remains on rollback.
- Supporting R-03-01/27/31: tenant predicates on every lookup, masked document/account views and reduced instructor data. The event/audit contract tests include the approved DataExported/DATA_EXPORTED values. E2 contract tests now exclude these implemented endpoints from 501 assertions and retain the metadata/schema checks.

### Assumptions
- E2-T01 is verified. E2-T06 has no census writers yet; this task activates list/facet/export readers on its existing contract controllers with a read-only adapter. E2-T06/S13 must align final persistence and extend inactivity/other lifecycle-derived state. This task derives basic status/scheduled leave, dog age and free-training values, without implementing those later workflows.
- R-03-23 and T-03-10's explicit shared-view read behavior govern the contradictory “INSTRUCTOR: només vistes pròpies” exception, interpreted as edit scope. Own/shared views are readable by instructors; others' shared views cannot be changed by them.
- `id` is included in each filter allowlist for R-03-24 selection exports. S03 §6's updated handlerName/handler fields are reflected in the dog list contract. Unknown requested columns/fields fail with existing INVALID_FILTER; retired saved columns are ignored on load. `fields` keeps id and selects top-level properties; required nullable nested fields are retained.
- The fixed size menu is 20/50/200/1000, default 50; larger integer requests are capped at 1000. Business date projections accept ISO strings and UTC BSON dates. LocalizedText accepts the current values-map storage and plain locale maps; labels use the request language then club default.
- A bounded 5001-row probe avoids loading an unbounded export. Queued records persist ownerAccountId, clubId, kind=LIST, listKey, format, columns, canonical filters/sort/q, locale and createdAt in export_jobs. E2-T08 owns processing, total/concurrent limits, status/download endpoints, retention and completion signaling. Its existing `/exports/{id}` controller stays a 501 contract stub. No DataExported/audit completion is emitted merely for queueing.
- Apache POI 5.5.1 and PDFBox 3.0.8 are pinned from their official release pages ([POI](https://poi.apache.org/download.html), [PDFBox](https://pdfbox.apache.org/download.html)); PDFBox's bundled Liberation Sans supplies the export font. Unsupported glyphs use `?`; long values wrap and continue across numbered pages. PDF rendering tests run headless on macOS.

### Questions / catalog proposals
No new parameter, event, notification or error code was introduced. No blocking questions. The shared-view ambiguity, contract alignment and E2-T06/E2-T08 handoff are recorded in roadmap/MESSAGES.md for @organizer. No other task file, ROADMAP.md or Organizer verification section was edited.
